Truncated sequential guaranteed estimation for the Cox-Ingersoll-Ross models
Mohamed BEN ALAYA a , Thi-Bao Trâm NGÔ b and Serguei PERGAMENCHTCHIKOV a

Abstract
The drift sequential parameter estimation problems for the Cox-Ingersoll-Ross (CIR) processes under the limited duration of observation are studied. Truncated sequential estimation methods for both scalar and two-dimensional parameter cases are proposed. In the non-asymptotic setting, for the proposed truncated estimators, the properties of guaranteed mean-square estimation accuracy are established. In the asymptotic formulation, when the observation time tends to infinity, it is shown that the proposed sequential procedures are asymptotically optimal among all possible sequential and non-sequential estimates with an average estimation time less than the fixed observation duration. It also turned out that asymptotically, without degrading the estimation quality, they significantly reduce the observation duration compared to classical non-sequential maximum likelihood estimations based on a fixed observation duration.



keywords

Cox-Ingersoll-Ross processes;
guaranteed estimation method;
truncated sequential estimation; parameter estimation; minimax estimation.

2010 MSC

1
Introduction

1.1
Motivations

In this paper, based on the sequential analysis approach, we develop
a new truncated estimation method based on observations within the time interval
[0,T]
of the Cox-Ingersoll-Ross (CIR) process defined
through the following stochastic differential equation

\mathrm{d}X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=(a-bX_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}})\mathrm{d}t+\sqrt{\sigma X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}}\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}},\quad X_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}=x>0,\quad 0\leq t\leq T\,,

(1)

where
a>0
,
b>0
and
\sigma>0
are fixed parameters and
(W_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}})_{\mathchoice{t\geq 0}{t\geq 0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\geq 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\geq 0$}}}
is a standard Brownian motion.
Similarly to

Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)
,
we consider the sequential estimation problem for the parameters
a
and
b
under the condition that the diffusion coefficient
\sigma
is known.
It should be noted that in this case
the process (
1
) is ergodic (see, for example, in
Ben Alaya and Kebaier (2010)
, for the details)
and
has the ergodic density which defined as

{\bf q}_{\mathchoice{a,b}{a,b}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,b$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,b$}}}(z)=\frac{\beta^{\alpha}}{\Gamma(\alpha)}\,z^{\alpha-1}\,e^{-\beta z}\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{z\geq 0\}}{\{z\geq 0\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{z\geq 0\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{z\geq 0\}$}}}\,,

(2)

where
\Gamma(z)=\int_{0}^{+\infty}t^{z-1}e^{-t}\mathrm{d}t
,

\alpha=2a/\sigma
and
\beta=2b/\sigma
.

The CIR model is very popular in many important applications such as interest rate modeling
(
Cox, Ingersoll and Ross (1985)
;
Lamberton and Lapeyre (1997)
), stochastic volatility stock markets
Heston (1993)
;
Berdjane and Pergamenshchikov (2013)
;
Nguyen and Pergamenshchikov (2017)
)
and, moreover, in
Pergamenchtchikov, Tartakovsky and Spivak (2022)

discrete versions of CIR processes are used
in the epidemic analysis. To obtain reliable statistical inferences within this model, estimating the unknown parameters with guaranteed accuracy properties is necessary in the non-asymptotic setting. It should be noted that for the model of type (
1
) the usual maximum likelihood estimators are nonlinear functions of observations and it is not clear how to study such functions directly on the fixed time interval.
For these reasons to overcome these dificulties
in
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)
;
Novikov and Shiryaev and Kordzakhia (2024)
, sequential guaranteed estimation methods were developed to estimate the parameters of the model (
1
) with fixed estimation accuracy.
Unfortunately, the proposed sequential procedures do not control the observation duration, which restricts their applications in many practical applications since, in practice, the duration of observation is usually bounded. For example, for the portfolio optimisation problems for financial markets with unknown parameters
in
Berdjane and Pergamenshchikov (2015)
, it is shown that to construct optimal and robust financial strategies, it is necessary to use guaranteed truncated sequential estimators that can provide a fixed known estimation accuracy over fixed time intervals. For statistical models in discrete time truncated sequential procedures are developed in

Konev and Pergamenshchikov (1990)
and for
the stochastic differential equations with the bounded diffusion coefficients
such procedures were proposed in

Konev and Pergamenshchikov (1992)
;
Galtchouk and Pergamenshchikov (2011)
;
Galtchouk and Pergamenshchikov (2015)
;
Galtchouk and Pergamenshchikov (2022)

for parametric and nonparametric problems.
Unfortunately, these results can not be applied to stochastic differential equations with unbounded diffusion coefficients
as, for example, (
1
). To study non-asymptotic estimation methods for such models, one needs to develop new analytic tools based on the special form of this process. The main goal of this paper is to develop truncated guaranteed estimation methods
for the coefficients
a
and
b
, in the both scalar and two-dimensional parameter cases,
on the basis of the observations
(X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}})_{\mathchoice{0\leq t\leq T}{0\leq t\leq T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0\leq t\leq T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0\leq t\leq T$}}}
of the process (
1
),
where the observation duration
T>0
is fixed in advance.

1.2
Main contributions

In this paper, for the first time, the truncated sequential guaranteed methods were developed for the models (
1
)
with unbounded diffusion coefficients. The proposed estimators for the parameters
a
and
b
have a guaranteed non-asymptotic mean square estimation accuracy, which is found in the explicit form. Moreover,
through the asymptotic analysis methods developed in
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)

it is shown that the proposed truncated guaranteed procedures are optimal in the minimax sense
for local and general quadratic risks. For the local risks, the optimality properties are established in the class of all sequential and non sequential estimators with the
observation duration less than
T
when
T\to\infty
.
It is important to emphasize here that it is also established that the proposed truncated procedures asymptotically, without deteriorating the estimation quality, significantly reduce the observation period compared to usual non-sequential estimations.
Moreover, for the general quadratic risk, the optimality properties are established in the class of all sequential procedures with mean observation time
not exceeding the mean observation time of the proposed truncated procedures.
It should be noted here that this class is sufficiently large since it includes all possible sequential procedures that can use more than
T
observations duration; only the mean observation duration has to be less than the mean observation duration of the proposed truncated procedures.
This means that any sequential procedure having the same mean observation duration can not improve the accuracy properties with respect to the proposed one.

1.3
Organisation of the paper

The rest of the paper is organized as follows. In Section
2
, we analyze the scalar truncated sequential estimation methods for the model (
1
). In Section
3
, we develop the two-step sequential estimation method
for the parameter vector
\theta=(a,b)^{\top}
in the model (
1
). In Section
3.2
,
we find conditions on the parameters of the process (
1
) which provide the optimality properties in minimax sense for the proposed sequential procedures. Section
4
presents the
concentration inequalities for the CIR process. Some important conclusions are given in Section
5
.
Appendix
A
contains some useful properties of the CIR process (
1
) and some auxiliary lemmas recalled from
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)
.

2
Scalar truncated sequential procedures

2.1
Guaranteed estimation

First, we consider the estimation problem for the parameter
b
in the process (
1
) in the case, when
a
is known, i.e.
\theta=b
.
In this case
{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}
is the expectation with respect to the distribution
{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}
of the process (
1
) with
a fixed parameter
a
and

b=\theta
. In this case
the Maximum Likelihood Estimator (MLE) for
\theta
(see, for example, in
Ben Alaya and Kebaier (2013)
) is
the non-linear function of the observations
defined as

\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\frac{aT-X_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}+x}{\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}s}\,.

(3)

To study the estimation problem
in a non-asymptoitical setting in the paper
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)
it is proposed the sequential
estimation
procedure
\delta_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}=(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}},\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}})

defined
as

\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}=\inf\left\{t>0\,:\,\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}s\geq H\right\}\textrm{ and }\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}=\frac{a\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}-X_{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}+x}{H}\,,

(4)

where
H>0
is a fixed threshold. To ensure that the duration estimate does not exceed the fixed period of time
T

we define the truncated sequential procedure

\widetilde{\delta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}=(\widetilde{\tau}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}},\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}})\,,

(5)

in which the alternative stopping time
\widetilde{\tau}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}
and the corresponding
sequential
estimator

\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}
are defined as

\widetilde{\tau}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}=\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\wedge T\quad\textrm{and}\quad\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}=\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}\,{\bf 1}_{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}},

(6)

where
x\wedge y=\min(x,y)
and the notation
{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{A}{A}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle A$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle A$}}}
states for the indicator of the set
A
.
Now,
for any compact
\Theta\subset]0,+\infty[
we denote

{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\frac{a}{{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\,,\quad{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}=\min_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\theta\quad\mbox{and}\quad{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}=\max_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\theta\,.

(7)

Moreover, we need the following threshold

{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}=3^{2m-1}\Big(2{\bf x}_{\mathchoice{2m}{2m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2m$}}}+\sigma^{m}\big(m(2m-1)\big)^{m}\,{\bf x}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}\Big)\,.

(8)

where
the parameters
{\bf x}_{\mathchoice{q}{q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle q$}}}
are defined in (
122
).

Theorem 2.1
.

For any
T\geq 1
,
0<H<{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T
and integer
m\geq 2
the sequential procedure (
6
)
possesses the following non-asymptotic mean square estimation accuracy

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq\frac{\sigma}{H}+\frac{T^{m}{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{({\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H)^{2m}}:={\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H,T)\,,

(9)

where
{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}={\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}{\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}/{\bf b}^{2m}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}
.

Proof.

Indeed,
followed by
(
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov 2025
, Theorem 1)
, we have

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}-\theta)^{2}{\bf 1}_{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}}+\theta^{2}{\bf P}_{\theta}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma}{H}+\theta^{2}{\bf P}_{\theta}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T).

(10)

From this, we note that for
0<H<{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T

{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T)={\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}s<H\right)\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(|{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}|>{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H\right)\,,

(11)

where
{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{T}\left(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}-a/\theta\right)\mathrm{d}s
.
Then, from (
11
) and the concentration inequality (
110
)
for

0<H<{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T
, we get

\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T\big)\leq\frac{\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}^{2m}}{({\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H)^{2m}}\leq\frac{T^{m}}{({\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H)^{2m}}\frac{{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{{\bf b}^{2m}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}\,,

(12)

where
{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}
is defined in (
8
).
Therefore, using this in (
10
) the estimation accuracy can be estimated as

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}-\theta)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma}{H}+\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}\theta^{2}\frac{T^{m}}{({\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H)^{2m}}\frac{{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{{\bf b}^{2m}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}\,.

This implies directly the bound (
9
).

∎

Now one needs to choose an optimal value for the parameter
H

to minimise the estimation accuracy (
9
), i.e.

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\argmin_{\mathchoice{0<H<{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T}{0<H<{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0<H<{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0<H<{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T$}}}{\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H,T)\,.

(13)

In this case we define the procedure

\big(\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\big)\,,\quad\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\widetilde{\tau}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}}\,.

(14)

Corollary 2.2
.

For any integer
m>1
the optimal truncated procedure (
14
) posses the following asymptotic
properties:.

1.

the optimal parameter
(
13
) is represented as

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}={\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-(2m{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}{{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}T^{\frac{2+m}{2m+1}}(1+\mathrm{o}(1))\quad\mbox{as}\quad T\to\infty\,;

(15)

2.

the corresponding optimal estimation accuracy
for any
m>1

has the following form

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq{\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T)=\frac{\sigma}{{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T}+\mathrm{O}\left(\frac{1}{T^{\frac{3m}{2m+1}}}\right)\quad\mbox{as}\quad T\to\infty\,.

(16)

Proof.

First note that to calculate the parameter (
13
) one needs to
study the equation

\frac{\partial}{\partial H}\,{\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H,T)=0\,.

Using the form of the function
{\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H,T)

defined
in
(
9
), the root of this equation can be represented as

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}={\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-(2m{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}\,(H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}})^{\frac{2}{2m+1}}{T^{\frac{m}{2m+1}}}.

(17)

Taking into account here that
H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}<{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T
the parameter
H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}

can be estimated from below as

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}>{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-(2m{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}{{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}T^{\frac{2+m}{2m+1}}={\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T\left(1-(2m{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}{{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{1-2m}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2m+1}}\right)\,.

(18)

Moreover, using this bound in (
17
)
the parameter
H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
can be estimated from above as

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}<{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-(2m{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}{{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}\,T^{\frac{2+m}{2m+1}}\left(1-(2m{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}{{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{1-2m}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2m+1}}\right)^{\frac{2}{2m+1}}\,\,.

(19)

Taking into account that
for any
m\geq 2
the fraction

{(2+m)}/{(2m+1)}<1

and using the bounds
(
18
) and (
19
) we can deduce that for sufficiently large
T

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}={\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-(2m{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}{{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}T^{\frac{2+m}{2m+1}}(1+\mathrm{o}(1))\,.

Therefore,
using this form in
the bound
(
9
), we the representation (
16
).

∎

Remark 1
.

It should be noted (see, for example, in
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)
that in this case the Fisher information is represented as

I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta)=\frac{a}{\sigma\theta}\quad\mbox{and}\quad I_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}=\min_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta)=I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}({\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}})=\frac{{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\sigma}=\frac{a}{\sigma{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\,.

(20)

and, therefore, the bound (
16
) can be represented as

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{I_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T}+\mathrm{O}\left(\frac{1}{T^{\frac{3m}{2m+1}}}\right)\quad\mbox{as}\quad T\to\infty\,.

(21)

Now, we consider the estimation problem for the parameter
a
in (
1
) when the coefficient
b
is known, i.e.
\theta=a
.
In this case the Maximum Likelihood estimator is given as

\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\frac{bT+\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\mathrm{d}X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}}{\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\mathrm{d}t}\,.

(22)

Similarly to
(
4
) we define
the sequential estimation procedure
\delta_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}=(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\,,\,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}})
with
H>0
for the parameter
\theta
as

\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}=\inf{\left(t:\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}\,X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}s\geq H\right)}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}=\frac{b\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}+\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}\,X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}}{H}\,.

(23)

An extension from this result is that we can define the following truncated sequential procedure
\widetilde{\delta}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=(\widetilde{\tau}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}},\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}})
where the stopping time
\widetilde{\tau}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}
and the associated estimator
\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}
are defined by

\widetilde{\tau}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}=\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\wedge T\quad\textrm{and}\quad\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}=\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}\ {\bf 1}_{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}}.

(24)

We need the following integral

\displaystyle\mu_{\mathchoice{a,\theta}{a,\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,\theta$}}}=\int_{\mathchoice{{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}}\,\varphi(z)\,{\bf q}_{\mathchoice{\theta,b}{\theta,b}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta,b$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta,b$}}}(z)\mathrm{d}z\,,\quad\varphi(x)=\min(x^{-1},{\bf r})

(25)

where
the density
{\bf q}_{\mathchoice{\theta,b}{\theta,b}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta,b$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta,b$}}}
is defined in
(
2
) and
{\bf r}\geq 1
is some threshold which will be specified later.
For any compact
\Theta\subset(\sigma/2,+\infty)
we set

\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}=\inf_{\theta\in\Theta}\,\mu_{\mathchoice{a,\theta}{a,\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,\theta$}}}\,,\quad{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}=\min_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\theta\quad\mbox{and}\quad{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}=\max_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\theta\,.

(26)

Similarly to Theorem
2.1
, we study the non-asymptotic properties of the procedure (
24
).

Theorem 2.3
.

For any
T>0
,
0<H<\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T
and compact
\Theta\subset(\sigma/2,+\infty)

the sequential procedure (
24
) for any
m\geq 2

possesses the following non-asymptotic mean square accuracy

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}-\theta)^{2}\leq\frac{\sigma}{H}+\frac{T^{m}{\bf r}^{2m}{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{(\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T-H)^{2m}}:={\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H,T)\,,

(27)

where

{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}=\frac{{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}^{2}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{\sigma^{2m}}\left(\frac{4\,e^{\beta}}{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}+\frac{2^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\Gamma_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}{\beta^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}\wedge\beta^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}}+\,\frac{{2^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}}\,}{\beta}\right)^{2m}\,,

in which
\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}=2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}/\sigma
,
\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}=2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}/\sigma
,
\Gamma_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}=\max_{\mathchoice{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}\leq\alpha\leq\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}\leq\alpha\leq\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}\leq\alpha\leq\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}\leq\alpha\leq\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}$}}}\,\Gamma(\alpha)
and
{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}
is defined in (
8
).

Proof.

First, note that
from
(
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov 2025
, Theorem 1)
it follows that

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}-\theta)^{2}\leq{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}-\theta)^{2}{\bf 1}_{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}}+\theta^{2}{\bf P}_{\theta}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T)\leq\frac{\sigma}{H}+\theta^{2}{\bf P}_{\theta}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T)\,.

(28)

To estimate the last term in this inequality note that
\varphi(x)=\min\big(x^{-1},{\bf r}\big)\leq x^{-1}
and, therefore, we can deduce that

{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T)={\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}^{-1}\mathrm{d}s<H\right)\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\varphi(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}})\mathrm{d}s<H\right)\,.

(29)

Now, to use the deviations in the ergodic theorem for the process (
1
) we set

\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\varphi)=\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\left(\varphi(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}})-\mu_{\mathchoice{a,\theta}{a,\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,\theta$}}}\right)\,\mathrm{d}s\,.

(30)

Then, from (
29
) and (
114
) for

0<H<\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T
and
m>1
, we have

\displaystyle{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T)

\displaystyle\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\mu_{\mathchoice{a,\theta}{a,\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,\theta$}}}T+\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\varphi)<H\big)

\displaystyle\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(|\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\varphi)|>\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T-H)\leq\frac{{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\varphi)^{2m}}{(\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T-H)^{2m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{T^{m}}{(\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T-H)^{2m}}\frac{{\bf r}^{2m}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{\sigma^{2m}}\left(\frac{4\,e^{\beta}}{\alpha}+\frac{2^{\alpha}\Gamma(\alpha)}{\beta^{\alpha}}+\,\frac{2^{\alpha}\,}{\beta}\right)^{2m}.

Therefore,

\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T)\leq\frac{T^{m}{\bf r}^{2m}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{(\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T-H)^{2m}\sigma^{2m}}\,\left(\frac{4\,e^{\beta}}{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}+\frac{2^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\Gamma_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}{\beta^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}\wedge\beta^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}}+\,\frac{{2^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}}\,}{\beta}\right)^{2m}\,,

(31)

where the parameters
\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}
,
\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}
and
\Gamma_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}
are defined in (
27
).
Using this bound
in (
28
),
we obtain (
27
).

∎

Now similarly to the definition
(
13
) we choose an optimal value for the parameter
H

to minimise the estimation accuracy (
27
), i.e.

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\argmin_{\mathchoice{0<H<\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T}{0<H<\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0<H<\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0<H<\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T$}}}{\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H,T)\,.

(32)

Using this parameter in (
24
), we obtain the following sequential estimation
procedure
\big(\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\big)
, in which

\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\widetilde{\tau}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}}\,.

(33)

Now,
we can show the following result.

Corollary 2.4
.

Assume that for some
0<\delta<1/2

{\bf r}=\mathrm{O}(T^{\delta})\quad\mbox{as}\quad T\to\infty\,.

(34)

Then, for any
m>(1-2\delta)^{-1}

the optimal truncated procedure
(
33
)
posses the following asymptotic
properties:.

1.

the optimal parameter
(
32
)
is represented as

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T-{\bf r}^{\frac{2m}{2m+1}}\,(2m{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}{\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}^{2}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}T^{\frac{2+m}{2m+1}}(1+\mathrm{o}(1))\quad\mbox{as}\quad T\to\infty\,;

(35)

2.

the corresponding optimal estimation accuracy
has the following form

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq{\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T)=\frac{\sigma}{\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T}+\mathrm{o}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right)\quad\mbox{as}\quad T\to\infty\,.

(36)

Proof.

First note that to calculate the parameter (
32
) one needs to
study the equation

\frac{\partial}{\partial H}\,{\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H,T)=0\,.

Using the form of the function
{\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H,T)

defined
in
(
27
) the root of this equation can be represented as

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T-{\bf r}^{{\frac{2m}{2m+1}}}\,(2m{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}\,(H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}})^{\frac{2}{2m+1}}{T^{\frac{m}{2m+1}}}.

(37)

Taking into account here that
H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}<\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T
the parameter
H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}

can be estimated from below as

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}>\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T-{\bf r}^{{\frac{2m}{2m+1}}}\,(2m{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}{\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}^{2}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}T^{\frac{2+m}{2m+1}}=\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T\,\check{\varpi}_{\mathchoice{T,m}{T,m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T,m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T,m$}}}\,,

(38)

where
\check{\varpi}_{\mathchoice{T,m}{T,m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T,m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T,m$}}}=1-{\bf r}^{{\frac{2m}{2m+1}}}\,(2m{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}{\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}^{1-2m}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}T^{-\frac{m-1}{2m+1}}
.
Moreover, using this bound in (
37
)
the parameter
H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
can be estimated from above as

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}<\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}T-{\bf r}^{{\frac{2m}{2m+1}}}\,(2m{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}{\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}^{2}}/\sigma)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}\,T^{\frac{2+m}{2m+1}}\left(\check{\varpi}_{\mathchoice{T,m}{T,m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T,m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T,m$}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{2m+1}}\,.

(39)

Taking into account
the condition (
34
)
and using the bounds (
38
) and (
39
)
we get the asymptotic equality (
35
).
Moreover,
using this form in
the bound
(
27
), we obtain the representation (
36
).

∎

Remark 2
.

It should be noted that we can estimate the parameter
\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}
from below. First of all note that for any

\theta\in\Theta

\mu_{\mathchoice{a,\theta}{a,\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,\theta$}}}=\frac{\beta^{\alpha}}{\Gamma(\alpha)}\int^{\infty}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\min\big(z^{-1},{\bf r}\big)\,z^{\alpha-1}\,e^{-\beta z}\,\mathrm{d}z\leq\frac{\beta^{\alpha}}{\Gamma(\alpha)}\int^{\infty}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\,z^{\alpha-2}\,e^{-\beta z}\,\mathrm{d}z=\frac{2b}{2\theta-\sigma}

and, therefore,

\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}\leq\frac{2b}{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma}\,.

Moreover, note also that
we can deduce the following inequality

\displaystyle\mu_{\mathchoice{a,\theta}{a,\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,\theta$}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\beta^{\alpha}}{\Gamma(\alpha)}\int^{\infty}_{\mathchoice{{\bf r}^{-1}}{{\bf r}^{-1}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf r}^{-1}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf r}^{-1}$}}}\,z^{\alpha-2}\,\mathrm{d}z=\frac{2b}{2\theta-\sigma}-\frac{\beta^{\alpha}}{\Gamma(\alpha)}\int^{{\bf r}^{-1}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,z^{\alpha-2}\,e^{-\beta z}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2b}{2\theta-\sigma}-\frac{\beta^{\alpha}}{\Gamma(\alpha)}\int^{{\bf r}^{-1}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,z^{\alpha-2}\,\,\mathrm{d}z\geq\frac{2b}{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma}-\frac{{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{{\bf r}^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}-1}}\,,

in which

{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\frac{\beta^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}\vee\beta^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}}{\Gamma_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}-1)}\,,

where

a\vee b=\max(a,b)
and
\Gamma_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}=\min_{\mathchoice{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}\leq\alpha\leq\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}\leq\alpha\leq\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}\leq\alpha\leq\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}\leq\alpha\leq\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}$}}}\Gamma(\alpha)
. Now
setting here
for any

0<\epsilon<1\wedge(2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma){\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}/(2b)

the threshold
{\bf r}
as

{\bf r}=\left(\frac{(2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma){\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{2b\epsilon}\right)^{\frac{1}{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}-1}}\,,

(40)

we obtain that

\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}\geq(1-\epsilon)\frac{2b}{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma}\,.

Therefore, choosing
\epsilon=T^{-\delta(\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}-1)}
for some
0<\delta<1/2
we obtain that for
T\to\infty

\mu_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}\to\frac{2b}{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma}\quad\mbox{and}\quad{\bf r}=\mbox{O}(T^{\delta})\,.

Note that the Fisher information in this case

I_{\mathchoice{b}{b}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}}}(\theta)=\frac{2b}{\sigma(2\theta-\sigma)}\quad\mbox{and}\quad I_{\mathchoice{b,*}{b,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle b,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b,*$}}}=\min_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}I(\theta)=I_{\mathchoice{b}{b}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}}}({\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}})=\frac{2b}{\sigma(2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma)}\,.

(41)

Therefore, from
(
35
) and
(
36
) it follows immediately that

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\frac{2b}{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma}T+\mbox{o}(T)\quad\mbox{as}\quad T\to\infty

(42)

and

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{I_{\mathchoice{b,*}{b,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle b,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b,*$}}}T}+\mathrm{o}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right)\quad\mbox{as}\quad T\to\infty\,.

(43)

Remark 3
.

Note that we will see later that
asymptotically, as
T\to\infty
, the bounds (
21
)
and (
43
) are minimal.

2.2
Optimality properties for the procedure (
14
).

Now let us consider the properties of stopping time that determines the duration of estimation in procedure
(
14
).

Proposition 2.5
.

For any compact set
\Theta\subset]0,+\infty[
the stopping time
\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
defined in
the procedure (
14
) for any
r>0

satisfies the following asymptotic property

\lim_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|\frac{\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{T}-\frac{\theta}{{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\right|^{r}=0\,.

(44)

Proof.

First note that for the stopping time
(
4
)
in
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)
it is shown that
any compact set
\Theta\subset]0,+\infty[
and any
r>0

\lim_{\mathchoice{H\to\infty}{H\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|\frac{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{H}-\frac{\theta}{a}\right|^{r}=0\,.

(45)

Note also that using the bound
(
12
) and the representation (
15
)
one can obtain that for any
m\geq 2

{{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}>T)=\mathrm{O}\left(\frac{1}{T^{\frac{3m}{2m+1}}}\right)\quad\mbox{as}\quad T\to\infty\,.

(46)

Now using this we obtain that for any
\theta
from
\Theta

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|\frac{\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{T}-\frac{\theta}{{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\right|^{r}

\displaystyle={\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|\frac{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}}{T}-\frac{\theta}{{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\right|^{r}{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\leq T\}}{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\leq T\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\leq T\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\leq T\}$}}}+{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|1-\frac{\theta}{{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\right|^{r}{\bf 1}_{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}>T\}}

\displaystyle\leq{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|\frac{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}}{T}-\frac{\theta}{{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\right|^{r}+{{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}>T)\,.

Then, the asymptotic equalities (
15
), (
45
) and (
46
) yield the property
(
44
).

∎

Now to study local optimality properties for sequential procedures
we set the local class of sequential procedures defined as

{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)=\left\{(\tau\,,\,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\,:\,\sup_{\mathchoice{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau\leq T\right\}\,,

(47)

where
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\in\Theta
and
\gamma>0
such that
\{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|\leq\gamma\}\subseteq\Theta
.

Theorem 2.6
.

For any
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}>0
the sequential procedure (
