) is pointwise optimal

\lim_{\mathchoice{\gamma\to 0}{\gamma\to 0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma\to (48)

Proof.

First note that as it is established in
(

the process (
1
)
for any
\theta>0

satisfies the LAN condition with the normalised coefficient

defined in (

).
Therefore, in view of Proposition
A.1
for

we obtain that for any
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}>0
and

\underline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})}\,.

(49)

Moreover, it should be noted also that
from the property (
21
)
it follows that

\displaystyle\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\,

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}\,I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta)\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq\max_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta)\,\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\,\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\max_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta)}{I_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}}=\frac{{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}{{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}\,.

(50)

Choosing here
\Theta=[\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}-\gamma\,,\,\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}{+}\gamma]
for

and taking into account that for this set

as
\gamma\to 0
,
we obtain that

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{\gamma\to 0}{\gamma\to 0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}}\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\,\sup_{\mathchoice{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta)\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq 1\,.

(51)

Now, note
that the function
I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta)
is continuous. Therefore,
for any
0<\epsilon<1
there exists some constant
\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}>0
such that
for all
0<\gamma<\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}

I_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}=\min_{\mathchoice{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta)\geq(1-\epsilon)I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})\,.

Therefore, for such
\gamma>0

\displaystyle\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\,

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathchoice{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}=\frac{\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\,\sup_{\mathchoice{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}\,I_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}}{I_{\mathchoice{a,*}{a,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a,*$}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\,\sup_{\mathchoice{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}\,I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}}{(1-\epsilon)I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})}\leq\frac{1}{(1-\epsilon)I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})}\,.

Taking here the limit as
\gamma\to 0
and then as
\epsilon\to 0
we can conclude that

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{\gamma\to 0}{\gamma\to 0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}}\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\,\sup_{\mathchoice{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})}\,.

(52)

This implies the equality
(
48
). Hence Theorem
2.6
.

∎

To study optimality properties over some arbitrairy compact set
\Theta
we use the the same way as
in
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)
, i.e.
for some family of
sequential procedures
\big({\tau}^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\big)_{\mathchoice{T>0}{T>0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T>0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T>0$}}}

such that for any parameter
\theta\in\Theta
the expectation

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\to+\infty
as
T\to\infty
we use the following class

\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\left\{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\,:\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,\frac{{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau}{{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}\leq 1\right\}\,.

(53)

Theorem 2.7
.

For any compact set
\Theta\subset]0,+\infty[
, the sequential procedure
(
14
) is asymptotically optimal in the minimax setting, i.e.

\lim_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\frac{\inf_{\mathchoice{(\tau,\widehat{\theta_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\,(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}-\theta)^{2}}{\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta)^{2}}=1\,,

(54)

where the class
\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
is defined in (
53
) through the stopping time
\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
introduced in (
14
).

Proof.

First note that the property (
44
) implies that for any
\theta\in\Theta

the expectation

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\to\infty
as
T\to\infty
.
It should be also added that thanks to the property
(
44
)
the condition
{\bf C}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})
in Section
A.1
holds true. Moreover, the condition
{\bf C}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})

for this case is established in

(
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov 2025
, Theorem 5.2.)
. Therefore, Theorem
A.2
with
k=1
from Appendix
implies that

\lim_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\inf_{\mathchoice{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,I_{\mathchoice{a}{a}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle a$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle a$}}}(\theta){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}-\theta)^{2}\geq 1\,.

Again using here the property (
44
) and the form of the Fisher information
defined in (
20
) we obtain that

\lim_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\inf_{\mathchoice{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,T\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}-\theta)^{2}\geq\frac{\sigma{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}{a}\,.

Now the bound (
16
) implies the optimality property (
54
).

∎

Remark 4
.

Il should be noted that Theorem 2.2 and
Theorem 5.2
from
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)

are shown under the condition
a>\sigma/2
. Indeed, these result hold true for any
a>0
.

2.3
Optimality properties for the procedure (
33
)

Now let us consider the properties of stopping time that determines the duration of estimation in procedure
(
33
).

Proposition 2.8
.

For any fixed
b>0
any compact set
\Theta\subset]\sigma/2,+\infty[
the stopping time
\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
defined in
the procedure (
33
) for any
r>0

satisfies the following asymptotic property

\lim_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|\frac{\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{T}-\frac{2\theta-\sigma}{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma}\right|^{r}=0\,.

(55)

Proof.

First of all note that for the stopping time defined in
(
23
)
in
(
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov 2025
, Theorem 2.7)
it is shown that for
any compact set
\Theta\subset]0,+\infty[
and any
r>0

\lim_{\mathchoice{H\to\infty}{H\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|\frac{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{H}-\frac{2\theta-\sigma}{2b}\right|^{r}=0\,.

(56)

Note also that using the bound
(
31
)
and the representation (
35
)
one can obtain that

{{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}>T)=\mbox{o}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right)\quad\mbox{as}\quad T\to\infty\,.

(57)

Now using this we obtain that for any
\theta
from
\Theta

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|\frac{\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{T}-\frac{2\theta-\sigma}{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma}\right|^{r}

\displaystyle={\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|\frac{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}}{T}-\frac{2\theta-\sigma}{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma}\right|^{r}{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\leq T\}}{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\leq T\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\leq T\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\leq T\}$}}}

\displaystyle+{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|1-\frac{2\theta-\sigma}{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma}\right|^{r}{\bf 1}_{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}>T\}}

\displaystyle\leq{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|\frac{\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}}{T}-\frac{2\theta-\sigma}{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma}\right|^{r}+{{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}>T)\,.

Then, the asymptotic equalities (
42
), (
56
)
and (
57
) yield the property
(
55
).

∎

Theorem 2.9
.

For any fixed
b>0
and
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}>\sigma/2
the sequential procedure (
33
) is point-wise optimal

\lim_{\mathchoice{\gamma\to 0}{\gamma\to 0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}}\,\underline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\frac{\inf_{\mathchoice{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}\,{\bf 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}\,{\bf T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}}=1\,,

(58)

where the class
{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)
is defined in (
47
).

Proof.

First note that as it is established in
(
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov 2025
, Theorem 5.3.)
the process (
1
)
for fixed

and any
\theta>\sigma/2

satisfies the LAN condition with the corresponding Fisher information

defined in (

).
Therefore, in view of Proposition
A.1
for
k=1

we obtain that for any
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}>\sigma/2
and
for any
\sigma/2<\gamma<\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}

\underline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\inf_{\mathchoice{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}-\theta)^{2}\geq\frac{1}{I_{\mathchoice{b}{b}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})}\,.

(59)

Moreover, it should be noted also that similarly to the bound
(
50
)
using the inequality
(
43
) one can deduce the following upper bound for the mean square accuracy

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\,\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}\,I_{\mathchoice{b}{b}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}}}(\theta)\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq\frac{\max_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}I_{\mathchoice{b}{b}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}}}(\theta)}{I_{\mathchoice{b,*}{b,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle b,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b,*$}}}}=\frac{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}-\sigma}{2{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}-\sigma}\,.

(60)

Choosing here
\Theta=[\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}-\gamma\,,\,\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}{+}\gamma]
for
\sigma/2<\gamma<\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}

and taking into account that for this set

{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}/{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}\to 1
as
\gamma\to 0
,
we obtain that

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{\gamma\to 0}{\gamma\to 0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}}\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\,\sup_{\mathchoice{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}I_{\mathchoice{b}{b}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}}}(\theta)\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq 1\,.

(61)

Now from here through the same way used in the inequality
(
52
) we obtain that

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{\gamma\to 0}{\gamma\to 0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}}\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\,\sup_{\mathchoice{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{I_{\mathchoice{b}{b}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})}\,.

From this and the lower bound
(
59
)
it follows
the property
(
58
). Hence Theorem
2.9
.

∎

Now using Proposition
2.8
and the form of the Fisher information
I_{\mathchoice{b}{b}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle b$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle b$}}}
given in (
41
)
similarly to Theorem
2.7
one can show the following result.

Theorem 2.10
.

For any
b>0
and any compact set
\Theta\subset]\sigma/2,+\infty[
, the sequential procedure
(
33
) is asymptotically optimal in the minimax setting, i.e.

\lim_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\frac{\inf_{\mathchoice{(\tau,\widehat{\theta_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\,(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}-\theta)^{2}}{\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta)^{2}}=1\,,

(62)

where the class
\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
is defined in (
53
) through the stopping time
\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
introduced in (
33
).

Remark 5
.

It should be noted that the properties
(
44
) and (
55
) imply that for
T\to\infty
the mean observations durations
{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-T\to-\infty
for
\theta\neq{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}
and

\theta\neq{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}
respectively. This means that to obtain the optimality properties with respect to the non-sequential estimation based on the fixed observations duration
T

the procedures (
14
) and (
33
) essentially
reduce the duration of observations.

3
Two-dimensional truncated sequential estimation method

3.1
Guaranteed estimation

Now we develop a truncated sequential estimation method
for the two dimension parameter

\theta=(a,b)^{\top}
from some compact
\Theta\subset]\sigma/2,+\infty[\times]0,\infty[
.
For this problem, it is convenient to represent the process
(
1
)
as

\mathrm{d}X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}={\bf g}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{\top}\theta\mathrm{d}t+\sqrt{\sigma X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}}\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\,,\quad 0\leq t\leq T\,,

(63)

where
{\bf g}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=\left(1\,,\,-X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\right)^{\top}
. Note that
in view of the results from
Ben Alaya and Kebaier (2013)

in this case this process is ergodic and one can show that

{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}
a.s.
for any
\theta\in]\sigma/2,+\infty[\times]0,\infty[

the following limit equalities hold true

\lim_{\mathchoice{t\to\infty}{t\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\to\infty$}}}\frac{1}{t}\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}s=\frac{2b}{2a-\sigma}:=f_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\lim_{\mathchoice{t\to\infty}{t\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\to\infty$}}}\frac{1}{t}\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}s=\frac{a}{b}:=f_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\,.

(64)

Therefore, setting

G_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}{\bf g}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}{\bf g}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}^{\top}\mathrm{d}s=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}s&-t\\[11.38109pt]
-t&\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}{X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}}ds\end{array}\right)\,,

(65)

we obtain that

\lim_{\mathchoice{t\to\infty}{t\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\to\infty$}}}\frac{1}{t}G_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=F=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}f_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}&-1\\[11.38109pt]
-1&f_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\end{array}\right)\qquad{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}-\mbox{a.s.,}

(66)

where the matrix
F=F(\theta)
is positive definite. To estimate the parameters
\theta
we use the sequential procedure
introduced in
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)
.
To do this first we will use the family of stopping times
\big({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{z}{z}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle z$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z$}}}\big)_{\mathchoice{z>0}{z>0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle z>0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z>0$}}}
defined as

{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{z}{z}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle z$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z$}}}=\inf\left\{t\geq 0:\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}\,X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\,|{\bf g}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}|^{2}\mathrm{d}s\geq z\right\}\,,

(67)

provided that
\inf\{\emptyset\}=+\infty
. It should be noted that the properties (
64
)
imply directly that
{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{z}{z}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle z$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z$}}}<\infty
a.s. for any
z>0
. Now for any non-random sequence
of non-decreasing positive numbers
(\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}
for which

\sum_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}\,\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}<\infty

(68)

we define the sequential procedures

\left({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}},\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}\right)_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}
as

{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}={\bf t}_{\mathchoice{\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}=G^{+}_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}{\bf g}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\,.

(69)

Here the matrix
G^{+}=G^{-1}
if the inverse matrix
G^{-1}
exists and
G^{+}=0
otherwise.
The number of these estimates required to construct a two-step sequential procedure is determined by the following stopping time

\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}=\inf\left\{k\geq 1:\sum_{n=1}^{k}{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{2}\geq H\right\}\,,

(70)

where
H
is a positive non-random threshold that will be chosen below and

{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\frac{1}{|G^{-1}_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}|\,\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})>0\}}{\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})>0\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})>0\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})>0\}$}}}\,.

(71)

Here
|\cdot|
denotes the Euclidean norm for the vectors and matrices and
\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G)
is the minimal eigenvalue of the matrix
G
. As is shown in
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)

for any
\theta\in]\sigma/2,+\infty[\times]0,\infty[

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}{\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}={\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\frac{1}{(|F^{-1}|\,\mbox{tr}\,F)^{2}}>0\qquad{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}-\quad\mbox{a.s.}\,,

(72)

i.e.
\sum_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}{\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=+\infty

and, therefore, for any
H>0
the moment (
70
) is finite a.s. Moreover,
setting

{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\max_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\big(|F^{-1}|\,\mbox{tr}\,F\big)^{2}\,,

(73)

we chose the sequence
(\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}

as

\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{cc}H\,,&\quad\mbox{for}\quad n\leq{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\,;\\[5.69054pt]
\kappa^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\,,&\quad\mbox{for}\quad n>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\,,\end{array}\right.

(74)

where
{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}=2{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H
,
and

(\kappa^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}
is an increasing sequence such that for all
n
it is bounded from below as
\kappa^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\geq n
and for some
constants
\varpi>1
and
0<\delta^{*}<1/2
,

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,n^{-\varpi}\,\kappa^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\,<\,\infty\quad\mbox{and}\quad\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,n^{-\delta^{*}}\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa^{*}_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}\,<\,\infty\,.

(75)

For example, we can take

\kappa^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=n^{\varpi}
and
\delta^{*}=(2-\varpi)/2
for some
1<\varpi<2
.

Now using this sequence one can show the following property for the moment
\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}
.

Lemma 3.1
.

For any compact set
\Theta\subset(\sigma/2,+\infty)\,\times\,(0,+\infty)
, for any
r>1
and
H>0

there exists some constant
{\bf v}^{*}_{\mathchoice{r}{r}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}}}>0
such that for any

n>{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H

the distribution tail of the stopping time (
70
)
can be estimated from above as

\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left(\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>n\right)\leq\,{\bf v}^{*}_{\mathchoice{r}{r}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}}}\,\frac{(2{\bf u}_{*})^{2r}H^{r}+n^{2\delta^{*}r}}{(n-{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H)^{2r}}\,,

(76)

where the costant
0<\delta^{*}<1/2
is given in the condition (
75
).

The proof of this lemma is given in Appendix.

In this case we define the aggregated sequential estimation procedure
(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}},\overline{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}})
as

\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}={\bf t}_{\mathchoice{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\overline{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}=\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}\,{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{2}\right)^{-1}\,\sum_{n=1}^{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}\,{\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}\,.

(77)

In this paper we use the truncated version of this procedure

(\widetilde{\tau}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}},\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}})
in which

\widetilde{\tau}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}=\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\wedge T\quad\textrm{and}\quad\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}=\overline{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\ {\bf 1}_{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}}.

(78)

Now, we study this procedure in non-asymptotic setting, i.e. for arbitraire fixed
H>0
and
T\geq 1
. To do this we need the following
functionals

\mu_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}=\frac{a}{b}+\int_{\mathchoice{{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}}\,\varphi(x)\,{\bf q}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(x)\mathrm{d}x\quad\mbox{and}\quad\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\inf_{\theta\in\Theta}\,\mu_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,,

(79)

where the ergodic density
{\bf q}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}
is defined in
(
2
) and the function

\varphi(x)=\min\big(x^{-1},{\bf r}\big)
in which the threshold
{\bf r}\geq 1
will be chosen later.

Theorem 3.2
.

For any compact set
\Theta\subset(\sigma/2,+\infty)\,\times\,(0,+\infty)
, for any duration of observations
T>1/\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
,
for any parameter
1\leq H<\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T
and
m\geq 2
the sequential procedure (
78
) possesses the fixed guaranteed estimation accuracy, i.e.

\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}-\theta|^{2}\,\leq\frac{(2{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}+\rho^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}})\sigma}{H}+\frac{T^{m}\,\theta_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}{\bf r}^{2m}Z_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{(\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H)^{2m}}+\,\frac{2^{4m+1}{\bf v}^{*}_{\mathchoice{2m}{2m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2m$}}}{\theta_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}}{H^{2m}}\,,

(80)

where
\rho^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}=\sum_{\mathchoice{n>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{n>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}\big(\kappa^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\big)^{-1}
,

\theta_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}=\max_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}|\theta|^{2}
, the coefficient
{\bf v}^{*}_{\mathchoice{2m}{2m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2m$}}}
is given in Lemma
3.1

and

Z_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}=2^{2m}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}\left(\frac{1}{{\bf b}^{2m}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2m}}\left(\frac{4\,e^{\beta_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}}{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}+\frac{2^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\Gamma_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}{\beta_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}\wedge\beta_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}}+\,\frac{{2^{\alpha_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}}\,}{\beta_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}\right)^{2m}\right)\,.

Proof.
First, note that that on the set
\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})>0\}
the sequential MLE
(
69
) can be represented as

\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}=G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}^{-1}\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}{\bf g}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}=\theta+\sqrt{\sigma}\,G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower
