0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}^{-1}\,\eta_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\eta_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}=\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,X^{-1/2}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\,{\bf g}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\,.

(81)

Using here the definition (
67
) and the properties of the stochastic integrals we obtain that

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\eta_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}|^{2}={\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\,|{\bf g}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}|^{2}\mathrm{d}s=\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\,.

(82)

Furthermore, in view of (
81
) we can represent the estimator (
78
) in the following form

\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}=\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}}{\sum_{n=1}^{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{2}}\ {\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}}{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}$}}}=\left(\theta+\sqrt{\sigma}\,\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\,\xi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\sum_{n=1}^{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{2}}\right){\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}}{\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\leq T\}$}}}

(83)

and
\xi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}={\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}^{-1}\eta_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}
. Note now that

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left|\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}-\theta\right|^{2}\leq\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left|\overline{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}-\theta\right|^{2}\ +|\theta|^{2}\ {{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T).

(84)

On the one hand, taking into account here the definition
(
71
) and the property (
82
), we get that

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\xi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\kappa^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\eta_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}|^{2}=\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,.

Then, from here through the
Cauchy-Schwarz-Bunyakovsky inequality and the definition
(
70
),
we find

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left|\overline{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}-\theta\right|^{2}\leq\,\sigma\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}|\xi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}|^{2}}{\sum_{n=1}^{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{2}}\leq\sigma\,\frac{1}{H}\,\sum_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\xi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}|^{2}\leq\sigma\,\frac{1}{H}\,\sum_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}\,\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,,

(85)

where in view of the definition (
74
)

\sum_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}\,\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\leq 2{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}+\sum_{\mathchoice{n>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{n>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}\,\frac{1}{\kappa^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}=2{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}+\rho^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\,.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T)

\displaystyle={\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}>T)={\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\big(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\big)\mathrm{d}s<\kappa_{\mathchoice{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\big(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+\varphi(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}})\big)\mathrm{d}s<\kappa_{\mathchoice{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}$}}}\right)\,,

where
x+\varphi(x)=x+\min(x^{-1},{\bf r})\leq x+x^{-1}
. Therefore,

{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T)\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\big(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+\varphi(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}})\big)\mathrm{d}s<H\right)+{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left({\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}\right)\,.

(86)

Now, similarly to
(
11
)
and
(
30
)
we set

{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{T}\left(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}-\frac{a}{b}\right)\mathrm{d}s\quad\mbox{and}\quad\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\left(\varphi(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}})-\mu_{\mathchoice{1,\theta}{1,\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,\theta$}}}\right)\,\mathrm{d}s\,,

(87)

where

\mu_{\mathchoice{1,\theta}{1,\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,\theta$}}}=\int_{\mathchoice{{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}}\,\varphi(x)\,{\bf q}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(x)\mathrm{d}x
.
Using these deviations and the definitions
(
79
) the integral in (
86
) can be estimated from below
for any
\theta\in\Theta

as

\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\big(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+\varphi(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}})\big)\mathrm{d}s=\mu_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}T+{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}+\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\geq\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T+{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}+\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\,.

Therefore, the first term in the r.h.s. of (
86
) for

1<H<\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T
and
\theta\in\Theta
can be estimated through the Chebyshev’s inequality
for
m>1

as

\displaystyle{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\big(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+\varphi(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}})\big)\mathrm{d}s<H\right)

\displaystyle\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\Big(\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T+{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}+\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}<H\Big)

\displaystyle\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\Big(|{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}|+|\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}|>\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H\Big)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2m}\frac{{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}|^{2m}+{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}|^{2m}}{\big(\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H\big)^{2m}}\,.

Using here inequalities
(
110
)
and (
114
)
we obtain that

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}

\displaystyle\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\big(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+\varphi(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}})\big)\mathrm{d}s<H\right)\leq 2^{2m}\left(\frac{1}{{\bf b}^{2m}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}+\frac{{\bf r}^{2m}}{\sigma^{2m}}{\bf s}^{*}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}\right)\frac{{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}T^{m}}{\big(\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H\big)^{2m}}\,,

where

{\bf s}^{*}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\left(\frac{4\,e^{\beta}}{\alpha}+\frac{2^{\alpha}\Gamma(\alpha)}{\beta^{\alpha}}+\,\frac{2^{\alpha}\,}{\beta}\right)^{2m}\,.

Taking into account here that
{\bf r}\geq 1
we can obtain that

{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\big(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+\varphi(X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}})\big)\mathrm{d}s<H\right)\leq\frac{{\bf r}^{2m}\,Z_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}T^{m}}{\big(\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H\big)^{2m}}\,,

(88)

where the coefficient
Z_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}
is defined in the bound (
80
).
Considering the second term in the r.h.s. of (
86
), using Lemma
3.1
, for any
r>1
and
H\geq 1
, we have

\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left(\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\right)\leq\,{\bf v}^{*}_{\mathchoice{r}{r}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}}}\,\frac{(2{\bf u}_{*})^{2r}H^{r}+{{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}^{2\delta^{*}r}}{({\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}-{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H)^{2r}}\leq 2^{2r}{\bf v}^{*}_{\mathchoice{r}{r}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}}}\,\left(\frac{1}{H^{r}}+\frac{1}{H^{2(1-\delta^{*})r}}\right)\,.

Tsking into account here that
0<\delta^{*}<1/2
and that
H\geq 1
we can estimate this probability as

\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left(\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}\right)\leq\,\frac{2^{2r+1}{\bf v}^{*}_{\mathchoice{r}{r}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}}}}{H^{r}}

(89)

and, therefore, using this in (
86
) we get

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>T)\leq\frac{T^{m}{\bf r}^{2m}Z_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{(\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H)^{2m}}+\frac{2^{2r+1}{\bf v}^{*}_{\mathchoice{r}{r}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}}}}{H^{r}}\,.

(90)

Now, from (
84
) and (
85
) it follows that for any
m>1
and
r>1

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\ |\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H,T}{H,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H,T$}}}-\theta|^{2}\leq\frac{(2{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}+\rho^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}})\sigma}{H}+\frac{T^{m}\,\theta_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}{\bf r}^{2m}Z_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{(\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H)^{2m}}+\frac{2^{2r+1}{\bf v}^{*}_{\mathchoice{r}{r}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}}}{\theta_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}}{H^{r}}\,.

Taking here
r=2m
we obtain the bound (
80
).

∎

Now we consider the main term in the mean square accuracy in (
80
) setting

{\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H,T)=\frac{2{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\sigma}{H}+\frac{T^{m}\,\theta_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}{\bf r}^{2m}Z_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{(\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-H)^{2m}}\,.

(91)

Now similarly to the definition
(
13
) we choose an optimal value for the parameter
H

to minimise this function, i.e.

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\argmin_{\mathchoice{0<H<\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T}{0<H<\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0<H<\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0<H<\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T$}}}{\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H,T)\,.

(92)

Using this parameter in (
78
), we obtain the following sequential estimation
procedure
\big(\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\big)
, in which

\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\widetilde{\tau}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T$}}}\,.

(93)

Now similarly to Corollary

2.4
we can show the following result

Corollary 3.3
.

Assume that for some
0<\delta<1/2
the parameter
{\bf r}
in the definitions (
79
)
such that

{\bf r}=\mathrm{O}(T^{\delta})\quad\mbox{as}\quad T\to\infty\,.

(94)

Then, for any
m>(1-2\delta)^{-1}

the optimal truncated procedure
(
33
)
posses the following asymptotic
properties:.

1.

the optimal parameter
(
92
) for
T\to\infty

is represented as

H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\mu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T-{\bf r}^{\frac{2m}{2m+1}}\,\left(m\mu^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}Z_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}/({\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\sigma))\right)^{\frac{1}{2m+1}}(T)^{\frac{2+m}{2m+1}}(1+\mathrm{o}(1))=\bar{\mu}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T+\mbox{o}(T)\,;

(95)

2.

the corresponding optimal estimation accuracy for
T\to\infty

has the following form

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\big(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta\big)^{2}\leq{\bf e}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}(H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}},T)+\mathrm{o}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2m}}\right)=\frac{2{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\sigma}{\bar{\mu}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}T}+\mathrm{o}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right)\,,

(96)

where

\bar{\mu}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\min_{\mathchoice{(a,b)\in\Theta}{(a,b)\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(a,b)\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(a,b)\in\Theta$}}}\,\mbox{tr}\,F
.

3.2
Optimality properties

Now we study the optimality properties for the procedure
(
93
). First we study the stoping moment
(
67
).

Proposition 3.4
.

For any compact set
\Theta\subset(\sigma/2,+\infty)\,\times\,(0,+\infty)
and for any
r>0

\lim_{\mathchoice{H\to\infty}{H\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H\to\infty$}}}\sup\limits_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|\frac{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{H}-\frac{1}{\mbox{tr}\,F}\right|^{r}=0\,,

(97)

where the matrix
F
is defined in (
66
).

Proof.

First we show that for any
\varepsilon>0

\lim_{\mathchoice{H\to\infty}{H\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\big|\frac{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{{H}}{{H}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{H}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{H}$}}}}{H}-\frac{1}{\mbox{tr}\,F}\big|>\varepsilon\right)=0\,.

(98)

To do this note that for
0<\varepsilon<\min_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}(\mbox{tr}\,F)^{-1}
can be represented as

{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\big|\frac{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{{H}}{{H}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{H}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{H}$}}}}{H}-\frac{1}{\mbox{tr}\,F}\big|>\varepsilon\right)={\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{{H}}{{H}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{H}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{H}$}}}>t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\right)+{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{{H}}{{H}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{H}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{H}$}}}<t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\right)\,,

where
t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}=((\mbox{tr}\,F)^{-1}+\varepsilon)H
and
t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}=((\mbox{tr}\,F)^{-1}-\varepsilon)H
. The first probability here can be estimated as

\displaystyle{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{{H}}{{H}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{H}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{H}$}}}>t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\right)

\displaystyle={\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(-\mbox{tr}\,\left(G_{\mathchoice{t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}$}}}-t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}F\right)>t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\mbox{tr}F-H\right)

\displaystyle\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(|\mbox{tr}\,\left(G_{\mathchoice{t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}$}}}-t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}F\right)|>\varepsilon\mbox{tr}F\,H\right)\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(|\mbox{tr}\,\left(G_{\mathchoice{t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}$}}}-t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}F\right)|>2\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,H\right)

where

\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\varepsilon\inf_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,\mbox{tr}\,F/2
.
Using here the definition of the matrix
F
in (
66
), we get

{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{{H}}{{H}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{H}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{H}$}}}>t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\right)\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\big|\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}$}}}\big|>\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H\right)+{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(|{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}$}}}|>\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H\right)\,,

where

\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}\left(X_{\mathchoice{u}{u}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle u$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle u$}}}^{-1}-f_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\right)\mathrm{d}u
and the deviation

{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}
is defined in
(
11
) for
\theta=b
.
From (
1
), by Itô’s formula, we have for any
t>0
,

\ln X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=\ln x+\frac{2a-\sigma}{2}\,\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}+\sqrt{\sigma}\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}\,X^{-1/2}_{\mathchoice{u}{u}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle u$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle u$}}}\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{u}{u}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle u$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle u$}}}

and, therefore,

\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=\frac{2(\ln{X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}}-\ln{x})}{2a-\sigma}-\frac{2\sqrt{\sigma}}{2a-\sigma}\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}\,X^{-1/2}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\,.

Using here the bound
(
122
)we get that for any
\theta\in\Theta

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{2}\,\leq\frac{12}{(2a-\sigma)^{2}}\left((\ln{x})^{2}+{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\ln{X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}})^{2}+\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{u}{u}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle u$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle u$}}}\mathrm{d}u\right)\,.

Now, taking into account here that for any
\epsilon>0

\sup_{\mathchoice{x>0}{x>0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x>0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x>0$}}}\frac{|\ln x|}{x^{\epsilon}+x^{-\epsilon}}<\infty\,,

one can conclude that

\Upsilon^{*}=\sup_{\mathchoice{t\geq 1}{t\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\geq 1$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,\frac{{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{2}}{t}\,<\infty\,.

(99)

From here and (
110
) one can deduce that for any
H>1
for which
t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\geq 1
, i.e. for
H\geq 1+\max_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\mbox{tr}F

\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{{H}}{{H}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{H}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{H}$}}}>t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\right)\leq\frac{(\Upsilon^{*}+{\bf D}^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})\max_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{\varepsilon^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H^{2}}\leq\frac{(\Upsilon^{*}+{\bf D}^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})\big(f^{*}+\varepsilon\big)}{\varepsilon^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H}\,,

(100)

where
f^{*}=\max_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,(\mbox{tr}\,F)^{-1}
. Therefore,

\lim_{\mathchoice{H\to\infty}{H\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H\to\infty$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{{H}}{{H}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{H}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{H}$}}}>t_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\right)=0\,.

Moreover, for any
\theta\in\Theta

\displaystyle{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{{H}}{{H}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{H}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{H}$}}}<t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\right)

\displaystyle={\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\mbox{tr}\,\left(G_{\mathchoice{t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}$}}}-t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}F\right)>H-t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\mbox{tr}F\right)

\displaystyle={\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\mbox{tr}\,\left(G_{\mathchoice{t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}$}}}-t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}F\right)>\varepsilon\mbox{tr}F\,H\right)\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(|\mbox{tr}\,\left(G_{\mathchoice{t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}$}}}-t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}F\right)|>2\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,H\right)\,.

Therefore,

{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{{H}}{{H}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{H}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{H}$}}}<t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\right)\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\big|\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}$}}}\big|>\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H\right)+{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(|{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}$}}}|>\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H\right)

and similarly to
(
100
)
we can conclude the following limit equality

\lim_{\mathchoice{H\to\infty}{H\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{{H}}{{H}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{H}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{H}$}}}<t_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\right)=0\,,

which implies (
98
). Now we need to show that for any
r>0

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{H\to\infty}{H\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,\frac{{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}{\bf t}^{r}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{H^{r}}<\infty\,.

(101)

To this end setting
\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}={\bf b}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}/{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}
this moment can be estimated as

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,{\bf t}^{r}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}

\displaystyle=r\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{\infty}t^{r-1}{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}({\bf t}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>t)\,\mathrm{d}t=r\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{\infty}t^{r-1}{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\mbox{tr}\,G_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\,<H)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq 2^{r}\gamma^{r}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H^{r}+r\int_{\mathchoice{2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H}{2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H$}}}^{\infty}t^{r-1}{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}\,X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}s+\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}\,X^{-1}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}s<H\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle 2^{r}\gamma^{r}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H^{r}+r\int_{\mathchoice{2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H}{2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H$}}}^{\infty}\,t^{r-1}{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(|{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}|>f_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}t-H\,\right)\mathrm{d}t\,.

Taking into account here that
f_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\geq 1/\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}

and using the bound
(
4.1
) we obtain that sufficiently large
H

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,{\bf t}^{r}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{r}\gamma^{r}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H^{r}+r\gamma^{2m}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}{\bf D}^{*}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}\int_{\mathchoice{2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H}{2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H$}}}^{\infty}\,\frac{t^{r-1+m}}{(t-\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H)^{2m}}\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq 2^{r}\gamma^{r}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H^{r}+r2^{r+m-2}\gamma^{2m}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}{\bf D}^{*}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}\,\left(\int_{\mathchoice{\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H}{\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H$}}}^{\infty}\,\frac{1}{x^{m-r+1}}\,\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{(2m-1)(\gamma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H)^{m-r}}\right)\,.

Choosing here
m>r
we obtain the property (
101
) which together with
the equality
(
98
) implies (
97
).

∎

.

0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left(\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}>{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\right)+{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}>T\right)\,.

Now, using the equality (
95
)
and the condition (
94
)
in the bound
(

)
we obtain that

\lim_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}>T\right)=0\,.

(103)

The bound (

)
and Proposition

combined with (

) imply the property (

).

∎

Theorem 3.6
.

The sequential procedure (

) is pointwise optimal, i.e for any parameter

\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\in(\sigma/2,+\infty)\,\times\,(0,+\infty)

(104)

wher the class
{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)
and the matrix

are defined in (

respectively.

Proof.

Note that according to Theorem 9 from

for any

)

.
Therefore, in view of Proposition
A.1
for

we obtain that for any

and for any

for which

\big\{\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\,:\,|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma\big\}\subset(\sigma/2,+\infty)\,\times\,(0,+\infty)

the following lower bound holds true

\underline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle (105)

Moreover, note that

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\big|F^{1/2}(\theta)\,(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta)\big|^{2}\leq\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\big|F^{1/2}(\theta)\,(\overline{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}-\theta)\big|^{2}\,+|F^{1/2}\theta|^{2}\,{{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}>T)\,.

(106)

To study the first term we use Theorem 3.3 from

Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)
according to which for any compact set
\Theta(\sigma/2,+\infty)\,\times\,(0,+\infty)

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\sup\limits_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}{\bf m}^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta)\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|F^{1/2}(\theta)(\overline{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}-\theta)\right|^{2}\leq 2\sigma\,,

(107)

where

.
Moreover, using here
Theorem 3.2 from

Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)
one can conclude that

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\sup\limits_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\frac{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\mbox{tr}\,F(\theta)}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|F^{1/2}(\theta)(\overline{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}-\theta)\right|^{2}\leq 2\sigma

and, therefore, in view of the representation (
95
)

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}T\,\sup\limits_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\frac{\bar{\mu}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\mbox{tr}\,F(\theta)}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|F^{1/2}(\theta)(\bar{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}-\theta)\right|^{2}\leq 2\sigma\,.

We choose here
\Theta=\{\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\,:|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma\}

and note that

\left|F^{1/2}(\theta)(\bar{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}-\theta)\right|^{2}\geq\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}\Big(F^{-1/2}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})F(\theta)F^{-1/2}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})\Big)\left|F^{1/2}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})(\bar{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}-\theta)\right|^{2}\,,

where

is the minimal eigenvalue of the matrix

. Therefore, taking into account that

F(\theta)\to F(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})
as

we obtain that

\lim_{\mathchoice{\gamma\to 0}{\gamma\to 0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma\to 0$}}}\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}T\,\sup\limits_{\mathchoice{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|F^{1/2}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta)\right|^{2}\leq 2\sigma\,,

which together with the lower bound (

∎

Now we study the minimax properties for the sequential procedure (

Theorem 3.7
.

For any compact set
\Theta\subset(\sigma/2\,,\,+\infty)\times(0\,,\,+\infty)

the
sequential procedure (
93
)
is asymptotically optimal in the minimax sense, i.e.

\lim_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf
