0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\big|\,\widetilde{F}^{1/2}\,(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}-\theta)\big|^{2}}{\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\big|\,\widetilde{F}^{1/2}\,(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle (108)

where the class
\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
is defined in (

) and the matrix
\widetilde{F}=\widetilde{F}(\theta)=F/\mbox{tr}\,F
.

Proof.

Note that the property (
102
) implies the condition
{\bf C}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})
. Therefore, using
Theorem

A.2
for
k=2

we obtain that

\displaystyle\underline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,

\displaystyle T\,\overline{\mu}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,\inf_{\mathchoice{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\,\widetilde{F}^{1/2}(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}-\theta)|^{2}

\displaystyle=\underline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\inf_{\mathchoice{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\,{\bf m}^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta)\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\,F^{1/2}(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}-\theta)|^{2}\geq 2\,\sigma\,.

(109)

Moreover, taking into account in (
106
) that the expectation
{\bf m}^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta)={\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\leq{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}
and

{\bf m}^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta)={\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\leq T
, we get that

{\bf m}^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\big|F^{1/2}(\theta)\,(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta)\big|^{2}\leq\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\big|F^{1/2}(\theta)\,(\overline{\theta}_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}-\theta)\big|^{2}\ +T\,|F^{1/2}\theta|^{2}\,{{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}}(\tau_{\mathchoice{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}>T)\,.

Now, Theorem 3.3 from

Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov (2025)
and the property (
103
)
yield the following upper bound

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,T\,\overline{\mu}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\,\widetilde{F}^{1/2}(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}-\theta)|^{2}=\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\,{\bf m}^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta)\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\,F^{1/2}(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\theta)|^{2}\leq 2\,\sigma\,,

which together with the lower bound (
109
) implies the property (
108
).

∎

Remark 6
.

It should be noted that the property (
102
)
imply that for
T\to\infty
the mean observations duration
{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-T\to-\infty
for

\bar{\mu}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\neq\mbox{tr}\,F
.
Therefore, as is noted in Remark
(
5
) the procedure (
93
) has the same property as the procedures (
14
) and (
33
),
i.e. to provide the optimality properties it essentially reduces the duration of observations
compared with
the non-sequential estimation based on the fixed observations duration
T
.

4
Concentration inequalities for the CIR models.

In this section we study the properties of the deviation in the ergodic theorem for the process (
1
).
First we study the deviation problem fir this process
with the fixed parameter
a
and
b=\theta
. First we study the deviation (
11
).

Theorem 4.1
.

For any compact set
\Theta\subset(0,+\infty)
and
for any
m\geq 1

{\bf D}^{*}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{T\geq 1}{T\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\geq 1$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\frac{{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}^{2m}}{T^{m}}\leq{\frac{{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}}{{\bf b}^{2m}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}}\,,

(110)

where the constants
{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}
and
{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}

are defined in (
7
)
and
(
8
) respectively.

Proof.

First note that from (
1
) that for any
T\geq 1
we can write the term
{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
as

{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\frac{X_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}-X_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\theta}+\frac{\sqrt{\sigma}}{\theta}\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{T}\sqrt{X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}}\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\,.

(111)

Now, by the upper bound (
122
), we obtain that for any
m>1
and
\theta\in\Theta
,

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}^{2m}

\displaystyle\leq 3^{2m-1}\,\left(\frac{X_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{2m}+{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}X^{2m}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\theta^{2m}}+\left(\frac{\sqrt{\sigma}}{\theta}\right)^{2m}\,\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{T}\sqrt{X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}}\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3^{2m-1}}{{\bf b}^{2m}_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}}\,\left(2{\bf x}_{\mathchoice{2m}{2m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2m$}}}+\sigma^{m}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{T}\sqrt{X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}}\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\right)^{2m}\right)\,.

(112)

Now, using here the upper bounds (
122
) and (
123
) we can get that

\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{T}\sqrt{X_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}}\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\right)^{2m}\leq{\bf x}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}\,(m(2m-1))^{m}\,T^{m}\,\,.

The use of this bound in (
112
)
for
T\geq 1

implies the inequality (
110
).

∎

Now to study the deviations of the form
(
30
)
for any continuous and bounded
{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}\to{\mathbb{R}}
function
\phi
we set the general form deviation as

\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\phi)=\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\left(\phi(X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}})-\mu_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\phi)\right)\mathrm{d}t\,,

(113)

where
\mu_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\phi)=\int_{\mathchoice{{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}}\,\phi(z)\,{\bf q}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(z)\mathrm{d}z
and the density

{\bf q}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}

is defined
(
2
)
for
\theta=(a,b)^{\top}
.

Theorem 4.2
.

For any compact set
\Theta\subset(\sigma/2,+\infty)
, for any
m\geq 1
and any continuous and bounded
{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}\to{\mathbb{R}}
function
\phi

\Delta^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{T\geq 1}{T\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\geq 1$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\frac{{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\phi)|^{2m}}{T^{m}}<\frac{\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2m}}{\sigma^{2m}}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\left(\frac{4\,e^{\beta}}{\alpha}+\frac{2^{\alpha}\Gamma(\alpha)}{\beta^{\alpha}}+\,\frac{2^{\alpha}\,}{\beta}\right)^{2m}\,,

(114)

where
{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}
is defined in (
8
) and
\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}}|\phi(u)|
.

Proof.

We use the method proposed in
Galtchouk and Pergamenshchikov (2007)
. According to this method we need to find a bounded solution
y(x)
of the differential equation

\frac{\sigma}{2}x\,\dot{y}(x)+(a-bx)y(x)=\widetilde{\phi}(x)\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widetilde{\phi}(x)=\phi(x)-\mu_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\phi)\,.

(115)

One can check directlyb that in this case such solution can be represented as

y(x)=-\frac{2}{\sigma\,x^{\alpha}}\int^{+\infty}_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\widetilde{\phi}(u)\,u^{\alpha-1}\,e^{-\beta(u-x)}\,\mathrm{d}u\,,\quad\alpha=\frac{2a}{\sigma}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\beta=\frac{2b}{\sigma}\,.

(116)

Note that the function

\widetilde{\phi}(u)
is bounded, i.e.

\sup_{\mathchoice{u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}}\,|\widetilde{\phi}(u)|\leq 2\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle u\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}}|\phi(u)|\,.

Using this we obtain that for all
x\geq 1

\displaystyle|y(x)|\leq\,\frac{4\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\sigma x^{\alpha}}\int^{+\infty}_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\,u^{\alpha-1}\,e^{-\beta(u-x)}\,\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\leq\,\frac{2^{\alpha}\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\sigma x^{\alpha}}\int^{+\infty}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,z^{\alpha-1}\,e^{-\beta z}\,\mathrm{d}z+\,\frac{2^{\alpha}\,\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\sigma x}\int^{+\infty}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{-\beta z}\,\mathrm{d}z\,

\displaystyle\leq\,\frac{2^{\alpha}\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\Gamma(\alpha)}{\sigma x^{\alpha}\beta^{\alpha}}+\,\frac{2^{\alpha}\,\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\sigma x\beta}\,,

i.e.

\sup_{\mathchoice{x\geq 1}{x\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x\geq 1$}}}|y(x)|\leq\frac{2^{\alpha}\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\Gamma(\alpha)}{\sigma\beta^{\alpha}}+\,\frac{2^{\alpha}\,\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\sigma\beta}\,.

In the case, when
0<x<1
taking into account, that

\int^{+\infty}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\widetilde{\phi}(u)\,u^{\alpha-1}\,e^{-\beta u}\,\mathrm{d}u=\int^{+\infty}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\phi(u)\,u^{\alpha-1}\,e^{-\beta u}\,\mathrm{d}u-\mu_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}(\phi)\int^{+\infty}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}u^{\alpha-1}\,e^{-\beta u}\,\mathrm{d}u=0\,,

we can rewrite the solution (
116
) as

y(x)=\frac{2e^{\beta x}}{\sigma\,x^{\alpha}}\int^{x}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\widetilde{\phi}(u)\,u^{\alpha-1}\,e^{-\beta u}\,\mathrm{d}u\,.

So, for
0<x<1

|y(x)|\leq\frac{4\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,e^{\beta}}{\sigma\,x^{\alpha}}\int^{x}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}u^{\alpha-1}\,e^{-\beta u}\,\mathrm{d}u\leq\frac{4\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,e^{\beta}}{\sigma\,\alpha}\,,

and, therefore, for any
\theta\in\Theta

y_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{x\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{x\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x\in{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}$}}}\,|y(x)|\leq\frac{\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\sigma}\left(\frac{4\,e^{\beta}}{\alpha}+\frac{2^{\alpha}\Gamma(\alpha)}{\beta^{\alpha}}+\,\frac{2^{\alpha}\,}{\beta}\right)\,.

(117)

In view of the Itô formula for the function
V(u)=\int^{u}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}y(x)\mathrm{d}x
and the equation (
115
), we obtain, that

\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\phi)=V(X_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}})-V(X_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})-\sqrt{\sigma}\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,y(X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}})\,\sqrt{X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}}\,\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\,.

Using now the moment inequality
(
122
), we get, that for any
m\geq 1

\sup_{\mathchoice{T>0}{T>0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T>0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T>0$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}|V(X_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}})|^{2m}\leq y^{2m}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{T>0}{T>0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T>0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T>0$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}X^{2m}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=y^{2m}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}{\bf x}_{\mathchoice{2m}{2m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2m$}}}\,.

Moreover, through the bound
(
123
)
we obtain that for any
\theta\in\Theta

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,y(X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}})\,\sqrt{X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}}\,\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\right)^{2m}

\displaystyle\leq\left(m(2m-1)\right)^{m}\,T^{m-1}\,y^{2m}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}X^{m}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\left(m(2m-1)\right)^{m}\,T^{m}\,y^{2m}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,{\bf x}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}\,.

Therefore, we can estimate the deviation (
113
) for
T\geq 1

as

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,(\Delta_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\phi))^{2m}

\displaystyle\leq 3^{2m-1}\left({\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,V^{2m}(X_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}})+V^{2m}(X_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})\right)

\displaystyle+3^{2m-1}\,\sigma^{m}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,y(X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}})\,\sqrt{X_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}}\,\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\right)^{2m}

\displaystyle\leq 3^{2m-1}\left(2{\bf x}_{\mathchoice{2m}{2m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2m$}}}+\sigma^{m}\,\left(m(2m-1)\right)^{m}\,{\bf x}_{\mathchoice{m}{m}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle m$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle m$}}}\right)\,y^{2m}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,T^{m}\,.

Using here the bound (
117
)
we obtain the inequality (
114
).

∎

5
Conclusion

In the conclusion, we emphasize that

•

The truncated sequential estimation procedures are constructed and non-asymptotic mean square accuracies
are obtained in
(
9
),
(
27
)
and
(
80
).
The properties for the mean observations durations are studied in Propositions
2.5
,
2.8
and
3.5
.

•

For the first time for continuous time statistical models,
the optimality properties for the truncated sequential estimation
procedures are established in the class of all possible sequential procedures with arbitrary bounded stopping times determining the duration of the observation.

•

To provide the optimality properties the proposed truncated sequential procedures use essentially fewer observations than classical non-sequential estimators based on the fixed non-random duration of observations
(see Remarks
5
and
6
).

Conflicts of interest statement

On behalf of all authors, the corresponding author states that there is no conflict of interest.

A
Appendix

A.1
Local Asymptotic Normality property

First we recall that a family of probability measures
({\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta,T}{\theta,T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta,T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta,T$}}})_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta,T>0}{\theta\in\Theta,T>0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta,T>0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta,T>0$}}}
with
\Theta\subseteq{\mathbb{R}}^{k}
is called to satisfy the Local Asymptotic Normality condition (LAN)
at a point
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\in\Theta

if there is a scaling
k\times k
matrix

\upsilon_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
going to zero as
T\to\infty
such that for
any
u\in{\mathbb{R}}^{k}
for which the point
\widetilde{\theta}=\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}+\upsilon_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}u
belongs to
\Theta
,
the Radon-Nikodym derivative has the following asymptotic representation

\ln\frac{\mathrm{d}{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\widetilde{\theta},T}{\widetilde{\theta},T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\widetilde{\theta},T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widetilde{\theta},T$}}}}{\mathrm{d}{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T}{\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T$}}}}=u^{\top}\,\xi_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}-\frac{|u|^{2}}{2}+{\bf r}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(u)\,,

(118)

where
|\cdot|
is the euclidean norm in
{\mathbb{R}}^{k}
,

\xi_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}\xrightarrow[T\to\infty]{\mathcal{L}({\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T}{\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T$}}})}\mathcal{N}(0,1_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}})\quad\mbox{and}\quad\sup_{\mathchoice{|u|\leq{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{|u|\leq{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|u|\leq{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|u|\leq{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}}|{\bf r}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(u)|\xrightarrow[T\to\infty]{{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T}{\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},T$}}}}0\quad\mbox{for any}\quad{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}>0\,.

Here,
1_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}
is the identity matrix of order
k
.

To study the point-wise optimality properties
for sequential procedures
we will use the lower bound obtained in

(
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov 2025
, Proposition 6.1)

for the class
(
47
).

Proposition A.1
.

Assume that, LAN holds for
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}
from
\Theta
with the scale matrix of the form
\upsilon_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=(I(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})T)^{-1/2}
, where
I(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})
is some positive definite matrix. Then,
for any
\gamma>0
for which
\{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|\leq\gamma\}\subseteq\Theta
, the following asymptotic lower bound holds true

\underline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\inf_{\mathchoice{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in{\cal H}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}},\gamma)$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\upsilon_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}^{-1}\,(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}-\theta)|^{2}\geq k\,.

(119)

This lower bound will be used to study local optimality properties, i.e. for small vicinities of
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}
.
To study optimality properties over some arbitrary compact set
\Theta\subset{\mathbb{R}}^{k}
for the class of sequential procedures defined in
(
53
)
we need the following conditions.

{\bf C}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})

There exists
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\in\Theta
, such that
\{|\theta-\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|<\gamma\}\subset\Theta
for all sufficiently small
\gamma>0
and

\lim_{\mathchoice{\theta\to\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\theta\to\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\to\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\to\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}}\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\left|\frac{{\bf m}^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta)}{{\bf m}^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})}-1\right|=0\,,

(120)

where
{\bf m}^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta)={\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}
.

{\bf C}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})

There exists
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\in\Theta
for which the LAN condition holds true
for the scale matrix of the form
\upsilon_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}=I^{-1/2}(\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})T^{-1/2}
in which
I(\theta)
is positive defined and continuous matrix for any
\theta
from some neighborhood of the point
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}

in
\Theta
.

In the sequel we will use the following lower for this class obtained in
(
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov 2025
, Theorem 6.4)
.

Theorem A.2
.

Assume that the conditions
{\bf C}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})
–
{\bf C}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})
hold true for some
\theta_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}
from
\Theta
. Then,

\underline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\inf_{\mathchoice{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(\tau,\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}})\in\Xi^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf m}^{*}_{\mathchoice{T}{T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}}(\theta)\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,|\,I^{1/2}(\theta)(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{\tau}{\tau}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\tau$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\tau$}}}-\theta)|^{2}\geq k\,.

(121)

A.2
Moment properties of the CIR process

Now we study the moment properties for the stable CIR processes

Lemma A.3
.

For any
q>-2a/\sigma
and compact set
\Theta\subset]\sigma/2,+\infty[\,\times\,]0,+\infty[

{\bf x}_{\mathchoice{q}{q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle q$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{t\geq 0}{t\geq 0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\geq 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\geq 0$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}X^{q}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}<\infty\,.

(122)

The proof is given in Proposition 3 from
Ben Alaya and Kebaier (2013)
.

Remark 7
.

It should be noted that
for
q>0

the bound (
122
) holds true
for any compact set
\Theta\subset]0,+\infty[\,\times\,]0,+\infty[
.

A.3
Properties of stochastic integrals

Now we give the upper bound for the moments of the stochastic integrals.

Lemma A.4
.

Let
(f_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}})_{\mathchoice{0\leq t\leq T}{0\leq t\leq T}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0\leq t\leq T$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0\leq t\leq T$}}}
be adapted process such that for some
m>1

{\bf E}\,\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f^{2m}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\mathrm{d}t\,<\infty\,.

Then

{\bf E}\,\left(\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\mathrm{d}W_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\right)^{2m}\leq\,(m(2m-1))^{m}\,T^{m-1}\,\int^{T}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,{\bf E}\,f^{2m}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\mathrm{d}t\,.

(123)

This lemma is shown in
(
Liptser and Shiryaev 2001
, Lemma 4.12)
.

A.4
Proof of Lemma
3.1

First of all we need the following result shown in

(
Ben Alaya, Ngô and Pergamenchtchikov 2025
, Lemma A7)
.

Lemma A.5
.

For any
r>2
and any compact set
\Theta\subset(\sigma/2,+\infty)\,\times\,(0,+\infty)
for the matrices
(
65
) and (
66
) the following property holds true

\mathrm{d}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{z\geq 1}{z\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle z\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z\geq 1$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left(\sqrt{z}\,\left|\frac{G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{z}{z}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle z$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{z}{z}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle z$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{z}{z}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle z$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{z}{z}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle z$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle z$}}}$}}}}{z}-\frac{F}{\mbox{tr}F}\right|\right)^{r}<\infty\,.

(124)

First of all, note that from the definitions (
71
) and (
72
) we can deduce directly that
{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\leq 1
and
{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\leq 1
. Therefore,

\left|{\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-{\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\right|\leq 2\left|{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\right|\leq 2\left|{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\right|\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})>0\}}{\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})>0\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})>0\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})>0\}$}}}+2\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})=0\}}{\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.26909pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})=0\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})=0\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.18835pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})=0\}$}}}\,.

Note here that on the set
\{\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})>0\}
the first difference can be estimated as

\left|{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-{\bf b}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\right|\,\leq\left|{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\right|\quad\mbox{and}\quad{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\frac{G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}}{\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}-\frac{F}{\mbox{tr}F}\,.

Moreover, note that for any
\theta\in\Theta

{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})=0\right)={\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}\left(\frac{G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}}{\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\right)=0\right)\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\left|{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\right|\geq\lambda_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\right)\,,

where
\lambda_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\min_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(F)/\mbox{tr}F>0
.
Using here the Chebyshev inequality and the bound (
124
) we can deduce that for any
r>2

\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\lambda_{\mathchoice{min}{min}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle min$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle min$}}}(G_{\mathchoice{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf t}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}})=0\right)\leq\frac{\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left|{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\right|^{r}}{\lambda_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{r}}\leq\frac{\mathrm{d}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\lambda_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{r}}\kappa^{-r/2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\,.

Therefore, for any
r>2
the random variables
\psi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\sqrt{\kappa_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}({\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-{\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}})

can be estimated from above as

\psi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}|\psi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}|^{r}<\infty

and in view of
the definition (
70
) one can deduce that for any
n>{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H
and
r>2

\displaystyle{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left(\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>n\right)

\displaystyle={\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left(\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,{\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}<H\right)\leq{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left(\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\frac{|\psi_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}|}{\sqrt{\kappa_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}>{\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}n-H\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{({\bf b}^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}n-H)^{r}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\left(\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\frac{|\psi_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}|}{\sqrt{\kappa_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}\right)^{r}\,.

Through the Hölder inequality the sum in the last expectation can be estimated as

\left(\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\frac{|\psi_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}|}{\sqrt{\kappa_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}\right)^{r}\leq\left(\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}\right)^{r-1}\left(\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\frac{|\psi_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}|^{r}}{\sqrt{\kappa_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}\right)\,.

Therefore, for
n>{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H

\sup_{\mathchoice{\theta\in\Theta}{\theta\in\Theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta\in\Theta$}}}\,{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{\theta}{\theta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\theta$}}}\,\left(\upsilon_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}>n\right)\leq\frac{\psi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{(n-{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}H)^{r}}\,\left(\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}\right)^{r}\,.

Using here the conditions (
74
)-(
75
), we obtain
that

\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}\leq\frac{{\bf n}^{*}_{\mathchoice{H}{H}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}}}{\sqrt{H}}+\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa^{*}_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}\leq 2{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\sqrt{H}+n^{\delta^{*}}\sup_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}\,n^{-\delta^{*}}\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa^{*}_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}}\,.

This implies
the upper bound (
76
).

∎
